  [32m✓[0m lockedForever precedence: a -1 gate is LOCKED-∞ even with a spent record present
  [32m✓[0m a spent record whose gate has already REOPENED reads EMPTY (the reap boundary)

  [32m✓[0m finished → GENERAL, independent of any card
  [32m✓[0m both families locked → GENERAL (two lock pills)

  [32m✓[0m GENERAL wins over RELATIVE (precedence): a finished row with a blocking card is still GENERAL

  [32m✓[0m spread-blocked row never affects a held HELP card (R1 is hinder-only)

  [32m✓[0m GENERAL still wins over a spread-blocked RELATIVE (finished takes precedence)

  [32m✓[0m fires regardless of which lap the pickup happened on (lap gating removed)

  [32m✓[0m dismisses the moment the item is fired (item -> 0) and marks it done

  [32m✓[0m ONCE PER ROUND: after a timed-out show it will not re-show, still holding

  [32m✓[0m dismisses the moment the driver drifts
  [32m✓[0m self-dismisses after showSeconds
  [32m✓[0m FTUE 2-lap round: fires on lap 1 (the driver's 2nd, final lap)
  [32m✓[0m never fires at the finish crossing (lap == totalLaps, gated by lap < totalLaps)
